|                                                                                                 |
| Default diffusion models for dopants/defects can be changed using the following command:        |
|                                                                                                 |
| pdbSet <mater> <dopant/defect> DiffModel <model>  (or)                                          |
| pdbSet <mater> Dopant          DiffModel <model>                                                |
|                                                                                                 |
| where <mater> is the bulk material and <model> is one of the following:                         |
| Constant, Fermi, ChargedFermi, Pair, ChargedPair, React, ChargedReact, ChargedEqulibrium,       |
| Granular, Equilibrium, Numeric.                                                                 |
|                                                                                                 |
|                                                                                                 |
| Default diffusion activation models for dopants can be changed using the following command:     |
|                                                                                                 |
| pdbSet <mater> <dopant> ActiveModel <model>                                                     |
| pdbSet <mater> Dopant   ActiveModel <model>                                                     |
|                                                                                                 |
| where <mater> is the bulk material and <model> is one of the following:                         |
| None, Solid, Precipitation, Transient, Cluster, ComplexCluster, BIC, ChargedCluster,            |
| Equilibrium, NeutralCluster.                                                                    |
|                                                                                                 |
|                                                                                                 |
| Default diffusion charge models for dopants can be changed using the following command:         |
|                                                                                                 |
| pdbSet <mater> <dopant/defect> ChargeModel <model>                                              |
| pdbSet <mater> Dopant          ChargeModel <model>                                              |
|                                                                                                 |
| where <mater> is the bulk material and <model> is one of the following:                         |
| None, DopantOnly, DopantDefect, Defect.                                                         |
|                                                                                                 |
|                                                                                                 |
| Default diffusion cluster models for defect can be changed using the following command:         |
|                                                                                                 |
| pdbSet <mater> <defect> ClusterModel <model>                                                    |
|                                                                                                 |
| where <mater> is the bulk material and <model> is one of the following:                         |
| None, Equilibrium, 311, FRENDTECH, Loop, 1Moment, 2Moment, LoopEvolution, Full, Transient.      |
|                                                                                                 |
|                                                                                                 |
| Default boundary conditions for dopants/defect can be changed using the following command:      |
|                                                                                                 |
| pdbSet <mater> <dopant/defect> BoundaryCondition <model>                                        |
|                                                                                                 |
| where <mater> is the interface material and <model> is one of the following:                    |
| HomNeumann, Natural, Dirichlet, Segregation, ThreePhaseSegregation, GrainBoundarySegregation,   |
| GrainGrainBoundarySegregation, BulkGrainBoundarySegregation.                                    |
|                                                                                                 |
|                                                                                                 |
|*** Please note that not every model parameter is available for every dopant, defect in each  ***|
|*** material.                                                                                 ***|

Params&Command.103 String {fproc run.only AdvancedThermalImplantModel { } {

# 3.2.4.1: dfactor for Sentaurus MC

fproc mcdfactor_Phosphorus { Energy Dose } {
    set dfSi [expr 4.5*($Dose+1.0e13)/($Dose+3.0e14)]
    if { [pdbGet  Si  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $dfSi }
    set dfGe 7.0
    set xMoleFraction "(Germanium/(0.5*((-2.332e22*Germanium+2.5e45)^0.5+5.e22)))"
    return "($xMoleFraction*$dfGe+(1-$xMoleFraction)*$dfSi)"
}

#fproc mcdfactor_BF2 { Energy Dose } { return 1.2 } 

fproc mcdfactor_Carbon { Energy Dose } { 
    return [expr 3.0e15/(2.0e15+$Dose)]
}

fproc mcdfactor_Silicon { Energy Dose } {
    set dfSi 1.8
    if { [pdbGet Si Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $dfSi }
    set dfGe 2.0
    set xMoleFraction "(Germanium/(0.5*((-2.332e22*Germanium+2.5e45)^0.5+5.e22)))"
    return "($xMoleFraction*$dfGe+(1-$xMoleFraction)*$dfSi)"
}


# 3.2.4.2: Parameters for Sentaurus MC

pdbSetDouble      Si As        surv.rat  0.9
pdbSetDouble      Si P         surv.rat  0.75
pdbSetDouble      Si B         surv.rat  0.225
pdbSetDouble      Si F         surv.rat  0.5
pdbSetDouble      Si C         surv.rat  0.45
pdbSetDouble      Si Ge        surv.rat  0.9
pdbSetDouble      Si Si        surv.rat  0.75

fproc AdvancedThermalImpPreProcess { Species Energy Dose } {

    set T [simGetDouble Diffuse temp]

    pdbSetDouble      Si As    surv.rat  [expr 1.00+1.06*tanh(-($T+0.)/250.)]
    pdbSetDouble      Si P     surv.rat  [expr 1.03+1.10*tanh(-($T+40.)/220.)]

    if { $Species == "Phosphorus" && $T>150 } {
    pdbSetDouble      Si       d.sim     [expr 0.50-0.001*($T-150)*$Energy/(9+$Energy)]
    pdbSetDouble      Si P     nloc.pre  [expr 0.44-0.001*($T-150)*$Energy/(9+$Energy)]
    }

    pdbSetDouble      Si B     surv.rat  [expr 0.623+0.41*tanh(-($T+90.)/50.)]
    pdbSetDouble      Si B     amor.par  [expr 0.50+0.50*tanh(-($T-300.)/80.)]
    pdbSetDouble      Si F     surv.rat  [expr 0.79+0.70*tanh(-($T+20.)/80.)]
    pdbSetDouble      Si C     surv.rat  [expr 0.51+0.70*tanh(-($T+0.)/110.)]
    pdbSetDouble      Si Ge    surv.rat  [expr 0.40+0.50*tanh(-($T-250.)/100.)]
    pdbSetDouble      Si Si    surv.rat  [expr 0.83+0.20*tanh(-($T+20.)/80.)]
} 


# 3.2.4.3: UserDiffPreProcess

fproc AdvancedThermalDiffPreProcess { } {

    if { [sel name=Arsenic Silicon present] && [sel name=ArsenicInt Silicon present] } {
        sel Si name=Arsenic_Init store value= ((Arsenic<Arsenic_Implant)?(0.05*Arsenic):(0.05*Arsenic_Implant))
        sel Si name=ArsenicInt   store value="ArsenicInt+Arsenic_Init"
        sel Si name=Arsenic      store value="Arsenic-Arsenic_Init"
        sel name=Arsenic_Init delete
    }
    if { [sel name=Boron Silicon present] && [sel name=BoronInt Silicon present] } {
        sel Si name=Boron_Init   store value= ((Boron<Boron_Implant)?(0.03*Boron):(0.03*Boron_Implant))
        sel Si name=BoronInt     store value="BoronInt+Boron_Init"
        sel Si name=Boron        store value="Boron-Boron_Init"
        sel name=Boron_Init delete
    }
}


# 3.2.4.4: Subroutines for setting ifactor and acinit

fproc ifactor_Arsenic { Energy Dose } {
    set T [simGetDouble Diffuse temp]
    if {$T<200} {
    	set ifSi [expr 1.0 + 8.0*$Dose/($Dose+1.0e14)*$Energy*$Energy/($Energy*$Energy+4.0)]
    } elseif {$T>300} {
    	set ifSi 1.0
    } else {
    	set ifSi [expr 1.0 + (300-$T)/100.*8.0*$Dose/($Dose+1.0e14)*$Energy*$Energy/($Energy*$Energy+4.0)]
    }
    if { [pdbGet Si Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $ifSi }
    set ifGe 1.0
    set xMoleFraction "(Germanium/(0.5*((-2.332e22*Germanium+2.5e45)^0.5+5.e22)))"
    return "($xMoleFraction*$ifGe+(1-$xMoleFraction)*$ifSi)"
}

fproc acinit_Arsenic { Energy Dose } {
    set T [simGetDouble Diffuse temp]
    if {$T<=150}       { set acSi 2.0e16 
    } elseif {$T>=450} { set acSi 3.1e19
    } else             { set acSi [expr 2.0e16+($T-150)/300.*3.1e19] }
    if { [pdbGet Si Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $acSi }
    set acGe [pdbGet Ge As AcInit]
    set xMoleFraction "(Germanium/(0.5*((-2.332e22*Germanium+2.5e45)^0.5+5.e22)))"
    return "($acSi*(1-$xMoleFraction)+$acGe*$xMoleFraction)"
}

fproc ifactor_Phosphorus { Energy Dose } {
    set T [simGetDouble Diffuse temp]
    if {$T<200} {
    	set ifSi [expr 1.0 + 1.0/$Energy]
    } elseif {$T>300} {
    	set ifSi 1.0
    } else {
    	set ifSi [expr 1.0 + (300-$T)/100./$Energy]
    }
    if { [pdbGet Si Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $ifSi }
    set ifGe 2.2
    set xMoleFraction "(Germanium/(0.5*((-2.332e22*Germanium+2.5e45)^0.5+5.e22)))"
    return "($xMoleFraction*$ifGe+(1-$xMoleFraction)*$ifSi)"
}

fproc acinit_Phosphorus { Energy Dose } {
    set T [simGetDouble Diffuse temp]
    if {$T<=50}        { set acSi 4.0e18 
    } elseif {$T>=450} { set acSi 1.4e19
    } else             { set acSi [expr 4.0e18+($T-50)/400.*1.4e19] }
    if { [pdbGet Si Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $acSi }
    set acGe [pdbGet Ge P AcInit]
    set xMoleFraction "(Germanium/(0.5*((-2.332e22*Germanium+2.5e45)^0.5+5.e22)))"
    return "($acSi*(1-$xMoleFraction)+$acGe*$xMoleFraction)"
}

fproc acinit_Boron { Energy Dose } {
    set T [simGetDouble Diffuse temp]
    if { $Dose<1e14 }       { set acSi 8.0e18
    } elseif { $Dose>1e15 } { set acSi 8.0e17
    } else                  { set acSi [expr 8.0e32/$Dose] }
    if {$T>=500}            { set acSi 5.0e19
    } elseif {$T>50}        { set acSi [expr $acSi+($T-50)/450.*(5.0e19-$acSi)] }
    if { [pdbGet Si Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $acSi }
    set acGe [pdbGet Ge B AcInit]
    set xMoleFraction "(Germanium/(0.5*((-2.332e22*Germanium+2.5e45)^0.5+5.e22)))"
    return "($acSi*(1-$xMoleFraction)+$acGe*$xMoleFraction)"
}

}}
Params&Command.104 String {fproc run.only impPostProcess_AdvCal { Species Name Energy Dose Model IFac VFac CDose {Suffix ""} } {

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3.3.1: calculate ifactor, vfactor, dfactor from implantation conditions --------------------

    set iname ${Name}_LastImp${Suffix}
    set vname ${Name}_LastImp${Suffix}

    set matList [mater]
    foreach mat $matList {

        if { [expr $IFac >= 0.0] } {
            set ifactor $IFac
        } else {
            if { [info procs ifactor_${mat}_${Species}] != "" } {
                set ifactor [ifactor_${mat}_${Species} $Energy $Dose]
            } elseif { [info procs ifactor_$Species ] != "" } {
	        set ifactor [ifactor_$Species $Energy $Dose] 
            } else {
	        set ifactor 1.0
            }
        }

        if { [expr $VFac >= 0.0] } {
	    set vfactor $VFac
        } else {
            if { [info procs vfactor_${mat}_${Species}] != "" } {
                set vfactor [vfactor_${mat}_${Species} $Energy $Dose]
            } elseif { [info procs vfactor_$Species ] != "" } {
                set vfactor [vfactor_$Species $Energy $Dose] 
            } else {
                set vfactor 0.0
            }
        }

        if { [pdbGetBoolean AdvCal_IFactorsVFactorsZero] } {
            set ifactor 0.0
            if { $Species == "Silicon" } { set ifactor -1.0 }    ;# to compensate for Si atoms
            set vfactor 0.0
        }

        if { [pdbIsAvailable ImplantData DFactor.Used] && 
             ([pdbGetDouble ImplantData DFactor.Used] >= 0.0) } {
            set dfactor [pdbGetDouble ImplantData DFactor.Used]
        } elseif { [pdbGetBoolean ImplantData MonteCarloImplant] } {
    	    if { $Model == "sentaurus.mc" && [pdbIsAvailable $mat $Species MCDFactor] } {
                set dfactor [pdbGet $mat $Species MCDFactor]
            } elseif { $Model == "sentaurus.mc" && [info procs mcdfactor_${mat}_${Species}] != "" } {
                set dfactor [mcdfactor_${mat}_${Species} $Energy $Dose]
            } elseif { $Model == "sentaurus.mc" && [info procs mcdfactor_$Species ] != "" } {
                set dfactor [mcdfactor_${Species} $Energy $Dose]
            } else {
                set dfactor 1.0
            }
        } else {
            if { [info procs dfactor_${mat}_${Species}] != "" } {
                set dfactor [dfactor_${mat}_${Species} $Energy $Dose]
            } elseif { [info procs dfactor_$Species] != "" } {
                set dfactor [dfactor_$Species $Energy $Dose]
            } else {
                set dfactor 1.0
            } 
        }
        
        if { [info procs AmModel_${mat}_${Species}] != "" } {
	    set AmModel [AmModel_${mat}_${Species} $Energy $Dose]
        } elseif { [info procs AmModel_$Species] != "" } {
	    set AmModel [AmModel_$Species $Energy $Dose]
        } else {
	    set AmModel "Damage"
        }


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3.3.2: update AcInit, AmInit from implantation conditions ----------------------------------

        if { [pdbIsAvailable $mat "Amorphous"] } {
            if { ![pdbGetBoolean $mat "Amorphous"] } {
     	        if { [info procs acinit_$Species] != "" } {
                    term $mat name=${Name}AcInit store eqn = "[acinit_$Species $Energy $Dose]"
                }
                if { [info procs acinit_${mat}_$Species] != "" } {
                    term $mat name=${Name}AcInit store eqn = "[acinit_${mat}_$Species $Energy $Dose]"
                }
                if { [info procs aminit_$Species] != "" } {
                    term $mat name=${Name}AmInit store eqn = "[aminit_$Species $Energy $Dose]"
                }
                if { [info procs aminit_${mat}_$Species] != "" } {
                    term $mat name=${Name}AmInit store eqn = "[aminit_${mat}_$Species $Energy $Dose]"
                }
            }
        }
    

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3.3.3: sum up ints, vacancies and damage from several implants -----------------------------
    
	if { [pdbIsAvailable $mat "Amorphous"] } {
	    if { ![pdbGetBoolean $mat "Amorphous"] } {

                # Profile shift for Int
                set offset i.plus.offset
                set oprof ${iname}
                set nprof ${iname}_IShift_
                if {[ProfileShift $mat $Species $offset $oprof $nprof]} {
                   set iname $nprof
                }

                # Profile shift for Vac
                set offset v.plus.offset
                set oldprof ${vname}
                set newprof ${vname}_VShift_
                if {$vfactor != 0.0} {
                   if {[ProfileShift $mat $Species $offset $oldprof $newprof]} {
                      set vname $newprof
                   }
                }

                sel z = "$ifactor * $iname + Int_Implant" $mat name=Int_Implant store
                sel z = "$vfactor * $vname + Vac_Implant" $mat name=Vac_Implant store
		
		if { $AmModel == "Damage" } {
		    sel z = "$dfactor * Damage_LastImp${Suffix} + Damage" $mat name=Damage
		} elseif { $AmModel == "+1" } {
		    sel z = "$dfactor * ${Species}_LastImp${Suffix} + Damage" $mat name=Damage
		}                

                if {[sel name=$nprof present]} { sel name=$nprof delete }
                if {[sel name=$newprof present]} { sel name=$newprof delete }

	    } elseif { [pdbIsAvailable $mat "Damage"] } {
                if { [pdbGetBoolean $mat "Damage"] } {
                    if { $AmModel == "Damage" } {
		        sel z = "$dfactor * Damage_LastImp${Suffix} + Damage" $mat name=Damage
                    } elseif { $AmModel == "+1" } {
		        sel z = "$dfactor * ${Species}_LastImp${Suffix} + Damage" $mat name=Damage
                    }
                }
            }
        } elseif { [pdbIsAvailable $mat "Damage"] } {
            if { [pdbGetBoolean $mat "Damage"] } {
                if { $AmModel == "Damage" } {
	            sel z = "$dfactor * Damage_LastImp${Suffix} + Damage" $mat name=Damage
                } elseif { $AmModel == "+1" } {
		    sel z = "$dfactor * ${Species}_LastImp${Suffix} + Damage" $mat name=Damage
                }
            }
        }
    }
}}
Params&Command.105 String {fproc run.only ctrimDamageModels_AdvCal { } {
    set matList [mater]
    foreach mat $matList {
	if { [pdbIsAvailable $mat "Amorphous"] } {
	    if { ![pdbGetBoolean $mat "Amorphous"] } {
            	set Threshold [pdbGetDouble $mat AmorpDensity]
            	set den [pdbGetDouble $mat LatticeDensity]
            	set ths [Min $den $Threshold]
            	sel z = "$ths * PD_LastImp" $mat name=Damage_LastImp store
            	sel z = PD+PD_LastImp name=PD
            	sel z = "(PD>1.0)?(1.0):(PD)" name=PD store
	    }
	}
    }
}}
Params&Command.106 String {fproc run.only UserPointDefectModel { Species Name Energy Dose Model IFac VFac CDose {Suffix ""} } {

    LogFile IL1 ""
    LogFile IL1 "  (tcl proc: UserPointDefectModel() in AdvCal)"
    LogFile IL1 ""

    if { [pdbGetBoolean ImplantData MonteCarloImplant] } {
        if { ($Species != "BF2" || $Name != "Fluorine") } {
            if { $Model == "crystaltrim" } {
                # based on ctrimDamageModels in Damage.tcl
                ctrimDamageModels_AdvCal
            }
            if { $Model == "sentaurus.mc" } {
                if { ![sel name=EnergyDeposition present] } {
                    sel z = 0.0 name=EnergyDeposition store
                }
                sel z = "EnergyDeposition_LastImp + EnergyDeposition" name=EnergyDeposition store
            }

            impPostProcess_AdvCal $Species $Name $Energy $Dose $Model $IFac $VFac $CDose $Suffix

            if { [pdbGetBoolean ImplantData UseCoImplant] } {
                CoImpPostProcess $Species $Energy $Dose
            }
        }
    } else {
        impPostProcess_AdvCal $Species $Name $Energy $Dose $Model $IFac $VFac $CDose $Suffix
    }
}}
Params&Command.107 String {fproc run.only _AdvCal::ImpPreProcess { Species Energy Dose Tilt Rotation Slice Mode } {

# For analytic implantation

# ChanDoseScaling is used for Dose < 1e12 (smaller than smallest table entry).
# Not used for higher doses in order to preserve backward compatibility in CoImplant model.

    if { $Dose >= 1e12 } {
        pdbSet ImplantData ChanDoseScaling 0
    } else {
        pdbSet ImplantData ChanDoseScaling 1
    }

# If a user specifies UseHighEnergyBoronTable, new tables in Taurus format will be turned on for B, if E>200keV.
# In this case, it is recommended to use the keyword "pai" in the implant statement.

    if { ![pdbGetBoolean ImplantData MonteCarloImplant] } {
        if { $Species == "Boron" && $Energy >= 200 && [pdbGetBoolean ImplantData B UseHighEnergyBoronTable] } {
            pdbSetBoolean ImplantData Boron TaurusAiiMode 1
            implant spec=Boron Silicon dualpearson damage angle.dependent eff.caplayer.thick=0.0           imp.table=Taurus/boron_in_silicon_2012 eff.channeling.supress
        }
    }

# For MC implantation

    pdbSetDouble     Si P  LSS.pre  [expr 1.2+15.0/($Energy+20.0)+3.0/($Energy+2.0)]

    pdbSetDouble     Si H  LSS.pre  [expr (1.76*$Energy*$Energy+170.0*$Energy+50400.0)/                   ($Energy*$Energy-80.0*$Energy+30000.0)]
    pdbSetDouble     Si H  scr.par  [expr 1.0-0.4*$Energy/($Energy+100.0)*(($Tilt>4.0)?1.0:($Tilt/5.0+0.2))] 

    pdbSetDouble     Si He LSS.pre  2.228
    pdbSetDouble     Si He scr.par  [expr 1.0-0.4*$Energy/($Energy+100.0)*(($Tilt>4.0)?1.0:($Tilt/5.0+0.2))] 

    if { $Species == "Boron" } {
        pdbSetDouble Si    d.sim    [expr 0.50-0.25/($Energy+1.0)]
        pdbSetDouble Si B  nloc.pre [expr 0.44-0.10/($Energy+1.0)]
    }
    if { $Species == "BF2" } {
        pdbSetDouble Si    d.sim    0.4
        pdbSetDouble Si B  nloc.pre 0.4
    }
    if { $Species == "C2B10H12" } {  ;# calibration for 2.67keV < E < 20keV
        pdbSet       Si B  surv.rat [expr (25.0/$Energy+0.25)]
        pdbSet       Si C  surv.rat [expr (25.0/$Energy+0.25)]
        pdbSetDouble Si    d.sim    0.33
        pdbSetDouble Si B  nloc.pre 0.37
    }

# For thermal implantation

    catch {AdvancedThermalImpPreProcess $Species $Energy $Dose}

# For power device simulation
# Return to standard models at the first implant, for which the dose exceeds AdvCal_StandardModelsThresholdDose.

    if { [pdbIsAvailable AdvCal_StandardModelsThresholdDose] } {
        if { $Dose > [pdbGetDouble AdvCal_StandardModelsThresholdDose] } {
    	     AdvancedPowerDeviceModeReset
    	}
    }
}}
Params&Command.108 String {fproc run.only _AdvCal::ImpPostProcess { Species Name Energy Dose Model IFac VFac CDose } {
    if { $Species == "Boron" && $Energy >= 200 && [pdbGetBoolean ImplantData Boron UseHighEnergyBoronTable] } {
  	impInitSpecies  "Boron"  "Default"                ;# back to default
    }
}}
Params&Command.109 String {fproc run.only _AdvCal::MCPostProcess { Species Name Energy Dose Model IFac VFac CDose MCIFac MCVFac } {

    pdbSetDouble     Si    d.sim    0.50                 ;# back to default
    pdbSetDouble     Si B  nloc.pre 0.44                 ;# back to default
             
    if { $Species == "C2B10H12" && $Name == "Hydrogen" } {
        set ifactor [ifactor_C2B10H12 $Energy $Dose]
        sel z = "Int_Implant-$ifactor*Hydrogen_LastImp-0.667*Carbon_LastImp" Silicon name=Int_Implant store
        solution add name=Hydrogen nosolve store         ;# to save CPU time
        pdbSet       Si B  surv.rat 0.225                ;# back to default
        pdbSet       Si C  surv.rat 0.45                 ;# back to default
    }
}}
Params&Command.11 String {term run.only Ox /Si name=IntInterfaceCStarFactorOED store add eqn = 1}
Params&Command.110 String {fproc run.only _AdvCal::DiffPreProcess { } {
    catch {AdvancedThermalDiffPreProcess}
    catch {AdvancedFluorineDiffPreProcess}
    catch {AdvancedNitrogenDiffPreProcess}
}}
Params&Command.111 String {fproc run.only AdvancedModels { } {

# ============================================================================================
# 4.1: Interstitial clusters: small clusters, 311 defects and loops

pdbSet            Si Int   ClusterModel      Full
pdbSet            Si Int   MultiClusterModel Full {2Moment Loop}

pdbSet            Si CLoop K311              {[Arr 65      5.5]} ;# 311 to loop transformation
pdbSet            Si CLoop KCLoop            {[Arr 1.5e-7 -1.5]} ;# loop growth
pdbSet            Si C311  DampFactor        2e12
pdbSet            Si CLoop DampFactor        2e12
pdbSet            Si I2    InitPercent       1.0       


# ============================================================================================
# 4.2: Boron model

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4.2.1: Boron interstitial clusters (BICs)

pdbSet            Si B  ActiveModel       ChargedCluster
pdbSetBoolean     Si B  EORTrap           0


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4.2.2: Realistic AmInit after B implant, works fine with ChargedReact (BIC) model ----------

pdbSet            Si B  AmInit            2.3e20


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4.2.3: 5-stream model for B diffusion ------------------------------------------------------

pdbSet            Si B  DiffModel         ChargedReact


# ============================================================================================
# 4.3: Arsenic 

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4.3.1: 5-stream model for As diffusion -----------------------------------------------------

pdbSet            Si As DiffModel         ChargedReact

  
# ============================================================================================
# 4.4: Phosphorus

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4.4.1: 5-stream model for P diffusion ------------------------------------------------------

pdbSet            Si P  DiffModel         ChargedReact


# ============================================================================================
# 4.5: Indium
 
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4.5.1: Complex In clustering model ---------------------------------------------------------
 
pdbSet            Si In ActiveModel       ChargedCluster
pdbSetString      Si In ClusterDefects    "Int"

pdbSetString	  Si In Int ClusterSizes        { {1 0}        {2 1}     {2 0}     {2 -1}    }

pdbSetArray       Si In Int ClusterFormE        { Indium  0.0  In2I 0.3  In2 -0.1  In2V -0.5 }
pdbSetDoubleArray Si In Int ClusterCharge       { Indium -1.0  In2I 0.0  In2  0.0  In2V  0.0 }
pdbSetDoubleArray Si In Int ClusterDegeneracy   { Indium  1.0  In2I 2.0  In2  2.0  In2V  2.0 }

pdbSetArray       Si In Int CaptureRadiusFactor { Indium  0.0            In2  1.0  In2V  1.0 }
pdbSetArray       Si In Vac CaptureRadiusFactor { Indium  0.0                                }

pdbSetArray       Si In IndiumInt CaptureRadiusFactor {    Indium  1.0                       }
pdbSetArray       Si In IndiumVac CaptureRadiusFactor {    Indium  0.0                       }


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4.5.2: Initial conditions after In implant, to be used with complex model ------------------

pdbSet            Si In   AcInit          2.0e18
pdbSet            Si In   AmInit          2.0e19
pdbSetDouble      Si In2I FractionCryst   1.0
pdbSetDouble      Si In2  FractionAmor    1.0

fproc ifactor_Indium { Energy Dose } { return 0.5 }


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4.5.3: 5-stream model for In diffusion -----------------------------------------------------

pdbSet            Si In DiffModel ChargedReact

pdbSetDouble      Si In Abs.Error 1e5
pdbSetDouble      Si In Rel.Error 1e-2


# ============================================================================================
# 4.6: Fluorine

AdvancedFluorineModel


# ============================================================================================
# 4.7: Carbon

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4.7.1: C trapping at EOR -------------------------------------------------------------------

pdbSetBoolean     Si C  EORTrap 0
 
pdbSetDoubleArray Si C EORCaptureRadius   {
    CarbonCLoop {2.0e-8}
    CarbonC311  {0.6e-8}
}

pdbSetDoubleArray Si C EOREmissionRate    {
    CarbonCLoop {[Arr 1.0e10 3.0]}
    CarbonC311  {[Arr 1.0e18 4.0]}
}

pdbSetArray       Si C EORForwardReaction { 
    CarbonCLoop {CarbonInt*[expr 1*3.1415*[pdbGetElement Si C EORCaptureRadius CarbonCLoop]]* ([expr [pdbGetElement Si C D 0]]/(IntNeutralStar*[expr [pdbGetElement Si C I ChargeStates 0]]))}
    CarbonC311  {CarbonInt*[expr 4*3.1415*[pdbGetElement Si C EORCaptureRadius CarbonC311 ]]* ([expr [pdbGetElement Si C D 0]]/(IntNeutralStar*[expr [pdbGetElement Si C I ChargeStates 0]]))}
}


# ============================================================================================
# 4.8: Safe numerics options (NegErrCntrl for first time step)

math FTS.NegErrCntrl
math AMS.NegErrCntrl

}}
Params&Command.112 String {fproc run.only AdvancedFluorineModel { } {

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4.6.1: Fluorine initialization, diffusion, clustering --------------------------------------

solution          name=Fluorine add ifpresent=Fluorine !negative store
term              Si name=BoronDiffFactor store eqn = "1"
    
pdbSet            Si     F DiffModel             ChargedReact
pdbSet            Si     F ActiveModel           ChargedCluster

pdbSetDoubleArray Si     F Int D                 { 0 {[Arr 2.0e-2   0.50]} }
pdbSetDoubleArray Si     F Int ChargePair        { 0 {[Arr 5.0e-25 -4.59]} }

pdbSetDoubleArray Si     F Vac D                 { 0 {[Arr 1.0      3.50]} }
pdbSetDoubleArray Si     F Vac ChargePair        { 0 {[Arr 1.0e-17 -1.95]} }

pdbSetDoubleArray Si     F Int ClusterFormE      { Fluorine 0.0  F2 -2.5  F2I -3.5  F3I2 -6.4 }


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4.6.2: Fluorine dose loss ------------------------------------------------------------------

pdbSet            Ox     F Dstar                 {[Arr 1.0e-5  2.0]}
pdbSet            Ox_Si  F BoundaryCondition     ThreePhaseSegregation
pdbSet            Ox_Si  F CMax                  1.0e20
pdbSet            Ox_Si  F TrappingRate_Silicon  {[Arr 3.0     3.9]}
pdbSet            Ox_Si  F EmissionRate_Silicon  {[Arr 3.0e-12 2.9]}
pdbSet            Ox_Si  F TrappingRate_Oxide    1.0e-28  
pdbSet            Ox_Si  F EmissionRate_Oxide    1.0e-25  
pdbSet            Ox_Si  F D                     { 0 0 }
pdbSet            Ox_Si  F Trap.Fi               1.0
pdbSetBoolean     Ox_Si  F UseTotalInterfaceTrap 0

pdbSet            Nit    F Dstar                 {[Arr 1.0e-5  2.0]}
pdbSet            Nit_Si F BoundaryCondition     ThreePhaseSegregation
pdbSet            Nit_Si F CMax                  1.0e20  
pdbSet            Nit_Si F TrappingRate_Silicon  {[Arr 3.0     3.9]}
pdbSet            Nit_Si F EmissionRate_Silicon  {[Arr 3.0e-12 2.9]}
pdbSet            Nit_Si F TrappingRate_Nitride  1.0e-28  
pdbSet            Nit_Si F EmissionRate_Nitride  1.0e-25  
pdbSet            Nit_Si F D                     { 0 0 }
pdbSet            Nit_Si F Trap.Fi               1.0
pdbSetBoolean     Nit_Si F UseTotalInterfaceTrap 0


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4.6.3: Fluorine dependent boron dose loss (B-F clusters at interface) ----------------------

solution name=BF add ifpresent = "Boron Fluorine" !negative store

pdbSetString      Ox_Si  BF    EquationProc     BF_eq
pdbSetDouble      Ox_Si  BF_k1       1.0e-30  
pdbSetDouble      Ox_Si  BF_k2 {[Arr 1.0e34  1.0]}
pdbSetDouble      Ox_Si  BF    minConc 1.0e2

pdbSetString      Nit_Si BF    EquationProc     BF_eq
pdbSetDouble      Nit_Si BF_k1       1.0e-30  
pdbSetDouble      Nit_Si BF_k2 {[Arr 1.0e34  1.0]}
pdbSetDouble      Nit_Si BF    minConc 1.0e2

fproc BF_eq { mat sol } {
    set m1 [FirstMat $mat]
    set m2 [SecondMat $mat]
    set k1 [pdbDelayDouble $mat BF_k1]
    set k2 [pdbDelayDouble $mat BF_k2]
    set eqn       "$k1 * (Boron * Fluorine * Fluorine - $k2 * BF)"
    term          $m1 /$m2 name=form_BF store add eqn = $eqn
    pdbSetString  $mat BF       Equation "ddt(BF) - form_BF"
    AddEqnTerm    $mat Boron    "form_BF"
    AddEqnTerm    $mat Fluorine "2*form_BF"
    AddToTerm     $mat BTotal   "BF"
    AddToTerm     $mat FTotal   "2*BF"
}


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4.6.4: Initial conditions ------------------------------------------------------------------

fproc ifactor_Fluorine { Energy Dose } {
    return 0.0
}

fproc dfactor_Fluorine { Energy Dose } {
    return 0.2
}

fproc ifactor_BF2 { Energy Dose } {
    return 1.0
}

fproc dfactor_BF2 { Energy Dose } {
    return [expr 2+1.e6/$Energy/$Energy/$Energy*$Dose/($Dose+1.e14)]
}

pdbSetDouble      Si     F    AcInit             1.0e21
pdbSetDouble      Si     F1I1 FractionCryst      0.0
pdbSetDouble      Si     F2I1 FractionCryst      1.0
pdbSetDouble      Si     F3I2 FractionCryst      0.0
pdbSetDouble      Si     F2I0 FractionCryst      0.0

pdbSetDouble      Si     F    AmInit             1.0e18
pdbSetDouble      Si     F1I1 FractionAmor       0.0
pdbSetDouble      Si     F2I1 FractionAmor       0.5
pdbSetDouble      Si     F3I2 FractionAmor       0.5
pdbSetDouble      Si     F2I0 FractionAmor       0.0

fproc AdvancedFluorineDiffPreProcess { } {
puts infproc
    if { [sel name=Fluorine Silicon present] } {
        sel Si name=FluorineInt store value="FluorineInt+Fluorine" 
        sel Si name=Fluorine    store value="0.0"
    }
}

}}
Params&Command.113 String {fproc run.only AdvancedNitrogenModel { } {

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4.9.1: Nitrogen initialization, diffusion, clustering --------------------------------------

solution          name=Nitrogen add ifpresent=Nitrogen !negative store

pdbSet            Si     N DiffModel             NeutralReact
pdbSet            Si     N ActiveModel           None

pdbSetDoubleArray Si     N Int D                 { 0 {[Arr 5.0e-2 0.56]} }

# Ns + I <----> Monomer
pdbSet            Si     N Int Kf                {[expr [DiffLimit Si Int 0.0]]} 
pdbSet            Si     N Int Bind              {[Arr 5e22 3.56]}

# Monomer + V <----> Ns
pdbSet            Si     N Vac Kf                {4*3.141592*[pdbGetDouble Si LatticeSpacing]*  [pdbGet Si N Int D 0]+[DiffLimit Si Vac 0.0]}
pdbSet            Si     N Vac Bind              {1.0/[Arr 5e22 3.56]}


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4.9.2: Nitrogen dimer diffusion, clustering ------------------------------------------------

pdbSet            Si     N      Dimer            NDimer

pdbSet            Si     NDimer D0               {[Arr 3.35e-4 2.38]}

pdbSet            Si     NDimer ClusterDefects   {Int Vac}
pdbSet            Si     NDimer Int ClusterSizes { {1 0} {1 1} }
pdbSet            Si     NDimer Vac ClusterSizes { {1 0} {1 1} }

# Monomer + Monomer <----> Dimer
pdbSet            Si     NDimer Kf               {2*4*3.141592*[pdbGetDouble Si LatticeSpacing]*  [pdbGet Si N Int D 0]}
pdbSet            Si     NDimer Bind             {[Arr 5e22 3.18]}

# Dimer + V <----> N2V
pdbSetDoubleArray Si     NDimer Vac KfCluster    { NDimer {[expr [DiffLimit Si {Vac NDimer} 0.5]]} }
pdbSetDoubleArray Si     NDimer Vac BindCluster  { NDimer {[Arr 5e22 1.80]} }

# N2V   + I <----> Dimer
# Dimer + I <----> N2I
pdbSetDoubleArray Si     NDimer Int KfCluster    { N2V    {[expr [DiffLimit Si Int 0.4]]}
                                                   NDimer {[expr [DiffLimit Si {Int NDimer} 0.1]]} }
pdbSetDoubleArray Si     NDimer Int BindCluster  { N2V    {[pdbGet Si Int Cstar]*                  [pdbGet Si Vac Cstar]/[Arr 5e22 1.80]}
                                                   NDimer {[Arr 5e22 2.65]} }      ;# calibration parameter

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4.9.3: Nitrogen dose loss ------------------------------------------------------------------

pdbSet            Ox     N Dstar                 {[Arr 1.0e-5  2.0]}
pdbSet            Ox_Si  N BoundaryCondition     ThreePhaseSegregation
pdbSet            Ox_Si  N Int                   Scale.PairSegregation_Silicon 1.0

pdbSetDouble      Ox_Si  N CMax                  5.0e15   
pdbSetDouble      Ox_Si  N TrappingRate_Silicon  {[Arr 1.5e-13 0.56]}
pdbSetDouble      Ox_Si  N EmissionRate_Silicon  0.0
pdbSetDouble      Ox_Si  N TrappingRate_Oxide    {[Arr 1.0e-22 2.0]}
pdbSetDouble      Ox_Si  N EmissionRate_Oxide    {[Arr 1.0e-24 1.0]}
pdbSetDoubleArray Ox_Si  N D                     { 0 0 }
pdbSetDouble      Ox_Si  N Trap.Fi               1.0
pdbSetBoolean     Ox_Si  N UseTotalInterfaceTrap 0

pdbSetDouble      Nit    N Dstar                 {[Arr 1.0e-5  2.0]}
pdbSet            Nit_Si N BoundaryCondition     ThreePhaseSegregation
pdbSetDouble      Nit_Si N CMax                  5.0e15   
pdbSetDouble      Nit_Si N TrappingRate_Silicon  {[Arr 1.5e-13 0.56]}
pdbSetDouble      Nit_Si N EmissionRate_Silicon  0.0
pdbSetDouble      Nit_Si N TrappingRate_Nitride  {[Arr 1.0e-22 2.0]}
pdbSetDouble      Nit_Si N EmissionRate_Nitride  {[Arr 1.0e-24 1.0]}
pdbSetDoubleArray Nit_Si N D                     { 0 0 }
pdbSetDouble      Nit_Si N Trap.Fi               1.0
pdbSetBoolean     Nit_Si N UseTotalInterfaceTrap 0


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4.9.4: Initial conditions ------------------------------------------------------------------

fproc ifactor_Nitrogen { Energy Dose } {
    return 0.0
}

fproc dfactor_Nitrogen { Energy Dose } {
    return 0.5
}

pdbSetDouble      Si     N      AcInit           3.0e19
pdbSetDouble      Si     NDimer FractionCryst    0.4
pdbSetDouble      Si     N2V    FractionCryst    0.3
pdbSetDouble      Si     N2I    FractionCryst    0.3

pdbSetDouble      Si     N      AmInit           0.0
pdbSetDouble      Si     NDimer FractionAmor     0.0                  ;# calibration parameter
pdbSetDouble      Si     N2V    FractionAmor     1.0                  ;# calibration parameter
pdbSetDouble      Si     N2I    FractionAmor     0.0                  ;# calibration parameter

fproc AdvancedNitrogenDiffPreProcess { } {
    if { [sel name=Nitrogen Silicon present] } {
        sel Si name=NitrogenInt store value="NitrogenInt+Nitrogen" 
        sel Si name=Nitrogen    store value="0.0"
    }
}

# ============================================================================================
# 4.9.5: Safe numerics options (NegErrCntrl for first time step)

math FTS.NegErrCntrl
math AMS.NegErrCntrl

}}
Params&Command.114 String {fproc run.only AdvancedSPERModel { } {

# Phase-Field Model for SPER
pdbSet            Diffuse SPER                 1
pdbSet            Diffuse SPER.Model           PhaseField

# Recrystallization velocity from G.L. Olsen, MSR 3 (1988)
pdbSet            Si      SPER PhaseTransWidth 0.003
pdbSet            Si      SPER Lambda.Fac      1.61
pdbSet            Si      SPER Relax.Rate      {[Arr 3.08e8 2.68]/[pdbGet Si SPER PhaseTransWidth]/  [pdbGet Si SPER PhaseTransWidth]}

pdbSet            Si      SPER R.Fac.Aniso     { 100 20.0 110 10.0 111  1.0 }
pdbSet            Si      SPER E.Aniso         { 100  0.0 110  0.0 111  0.0 }
pdbSet            Si      SPER R.Fac           "(0.8+0.2*3.e18/(3.e18+Fluorine))"

pdbSet            Si      B    SPER.Energy     0.0
pdbSet            Si      B    DAmor           {[Arr 1.0    2.68]}
pdbSet            Si      As   SPER.Energy     4.0
pdbSet            Si      As   DAmor           {[Arr 0.4    2.68]}
pdbSet            Si      P    SPER.Energy     1.5
pdbSet            Si      P    DAmor           {[Arr 0.4    2.68]}
pdbSet            Si      In   SPER.Energy     1.5
pdbSet            Si      In   DAmor           {[Arr 1.0    2.57]}
pdbSet            Si      C    SPER.Energy     0.12
pdbSet            Si      C    DAmor           {[Arr 40     2.60]}
pdbSet            Si      F    SPER.Energy     0.35
pdbSet            Si      F    DAmor           {[Arr 12     2.58]}
pdbSet            Si      Ge   SPER.Energy     0.018
pdbSet            Si      Ge   DAmor           {[Arr 5.0    2.60]}

}}
Params&Command.115 String {fproc run.only AdvancedMLAModel { } {

pdbSet            Heat    Use.Melting.Laser               1

pdbSet            Heat    UpdateHeatRate                  0         ;# default
pdbSet            Heat    SeedOnPhase                     0.002     ;# default 1.0
pdbSet            Heat    Correct.Energy.Dose             1         ;# default 0

# Correction of Liquid.ThermalConductivity to account for the MassDensity change (for 1D)
pdbSet            Si      Liquid.ThermalConductivity                                          {(2.57/2.33)*(0.56+3.28e-4*(Temperature-[pdbGet Si Melting.Point]))}

pdbSet            Si      FV.Amorphous.Melting.Velocity.0 5.45e4    ;# default
pdbSet            Si      FV.Amorphous.Melting.Velocity.E 0.082     ;# default
pdbSet            Si      FV.Amorphous.Melting.Velocity.T 1040      ;# default
pdbSet            Si      FV.Melting.Velocity.0           3.27e4    ;# default
pdbSet            Si      FV.Melting.Velocity.E           0.082     ;# default
pdbSet            Si      FV.Melting.Velocity.T           1040      ;# default

pdbSet            Si      B 	Dliquid.0                 2.6e-4    ;# default
pdbSet            Si      B 	Dliquid.E                 0.0       ;# default
pdbSet            Si      B 	Dils.0                    1.0e-9    ;# default
pdbSet            Si      B 	Dils.E                    0.0       ;# default
pdbSet            Si      B 	Melting.Seg.E             0.033     ;# default
pdbSet            Si      B 	Melting.Intf.Seg.E       -0.15      ;# default
pdbSet            Si      P 	Dliquid.0                 3.0e-4    ;# default
pdbSet            Si      P 	Dliquid.E                 0.0       ;# default
pdbSet            Si      P 	Dils.0                    1.0e-9    ;# default
pdbSet            Si      P 	Dils.E                    0.0       ;# default
pdbSet            Si      P 	Melting.Seg.E             0.145     ;# default
pdbSet            Si      P 	Melting.Intf.Seg.E       -0.11      ;# default

pdbSetString      Si      Int SavedClusterModel           [pdbGet Si Int ClusterModel]
pdbSet            Si      Int ClusterModel                None
pdbSetBoolean     Defect  ForcedTurnOff                   1

# Time step controls
pdbSet            Heat    MaxTimeStep                     1.0e-9    ;# <s>
pdbSet            Si      Temperature Abs.Error           1e-8
pdbSet            Si      Temperature Rel.Error           1e-6
pdbSetDouble      Si      Temperature Transient.Rel.Error 3.0e-6
pdbSetDouble      Si      Dopant      Abs.Error           1e-3
pdbSetDouble      Si      Dopant      Rel.Error           1e-8
pdbSetDouble      Si      Dopant      Transient.Rel.Error 1e-5

pdbSet            Math    NegErrCntrl 1
math              fullNewton

}}
Params&Command.116 String {fproc run.only AdvancedMLAModelReset { } {

if { ![pdbIsAvailable Si Int SavedClusterModel] } { return }

pdbSet            Heat    Use.Melting.Laser               0
pdbSet            Heat    Correct.Energy.Dose             0

pdbSet            Si      Int ClusterModel                [pdbGet Si Int SavedClusterModel]
pdbUnSetString    Si      Int SavedClusterModel
pdbUnSetBoolean   Defect  ForcedTurnOff

pdbSet            Si      Temperature Abs.Error           0.3
pdbSet            Si      Temperature Rel.Error           1e-3
pdbUnSetDouble    Si      Temperature Transient.Rel.Error
pdbUnSetDouble    Si      Dopant      Abs.Error
pdbUnSetDouble    Si      Dopant      Rel.Error
pdbUnSetDouble    Si      Dopant      Transient.Rel.Error

}}
Params&Command.117 String {fproc run.only AdvancedPowerDeviceMode { { StandardModelsThresholdDose 1e30 } } {

LogFile "- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -"
LogFile "AdvancedPowerDeviceMode"
LogFile !PrintCommands

# The argument will be used in _AdvCal::ImpPreProcess to return to StandardModels
# automatically at an implant step with Dose > StandardModelsThresholdDose.

pdbSetDouble      AdvCal_StandardModelsThresholdDose $StandardModelsThresholdDose

# Numerics: Time steps
pdbSetDouble      Diffuse InitTimeStep        1.0e-3   ;# default 1.0e-4. 2.0 faster/less robust.
pdbSet            Diffuse IncreaseRatio       4.0      ;# default 2.0
pdbSet            Diffuse ReduceRatio         0.20     ;# default 0.25
pdbSet            Math    Time.Step.Function  Linear   ;# default Damped

# Numerics: Deposition steps
pdbSet            Diffuse dThickness          0.001    ;# default 0.001. 0.002 faster/less robust.
pdbSet            Diffuse dThicknessEpi       0.50     ;# default 0.1

# Numerics: Temperature steps
pdbSet            Diffuse MaxGrowthStep       50.0     ;# default 4.0
pdbSet            Diffuse delT                50.0     ;# default 10
pdbSet            Diffuse delNT               50.0     ;# default 50
pdbSet            Diffuse delTox              50.0     ;# default 50

# Physics: No dopant clusters
#          Justified only for dopant concentrations << solubility
pdbSet            Si B    ActiveModel         None
pdbSet            Si P    ActiveModel         None
pdbSet            Si As   ActiveModel         None
pdbSet            Si In   ActiveModel         None
pdbSet            Si Sb   ActiveModel         None

# Physics: No interstitial clusters, all ifactors and vfactors 0
#          Justified for high thermal budgets (Thermal diffusion >> TED)
pdbSet            Si Int  ClusterModel        None
pdbSetBoolean     AdvCal_IFactorsVFactorsZero 1

# Numerics: Errors for point defects
pdbSetDouble      Si Int  Abs.Error           {[expr [pdbGet Si Int Cstar]/1000.]} ;# def. 1e7
pdbSetDouble      Si Int  Rel.Error           1.0e-3                               ;# def. 1e-4
pdbSetDouble      Si Int  Transient.Rel.Error 1.0e-1                               ;# def. 1e-2
pdbSetDouble      Si Vac  Abs.Error           {[expr [pdbGet Si Vac Cstar]/1000.]} ;# def. 1e7
pdbSetDouble      Si Vac  Rel.Error           1.0e-3                               ;# def. 1e-4
pdbSetDouble      Si Vac  Transient.Rel.Error 1.0e-1                               ;# def. 1e-2

LogFile PrintCommands
LogFile "- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -"
}}
Params&Command.118 String {fproc run.only AdvancedPowerDeviceModeReset { } {

LogFile "- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -"
LogFile "AdvancedPowerDeviceModeReset"
LogFile !PrintCommands

# Do not call this procedure again automatically
pdbSetDouble      AdvCal_StandardModelsThresholdDose 1e30

# Numerics: Time steps
pdbSetDouble      Diffuse InitTimeStep        1.0e-4      ;# default 1.0e-4
pdbSet            Diffuse IncreaseRatio       2.0         ;# default 2.0
pdbSet            Diffuse ReduceRatio         0.25        ;# default 0.25
pdbSet            Math    Time.Step.Function  Damped      ;# default Damped

# Numerics: Deposition steps
pdbSet            Diffuse dThickness          0.001       ;# default 0.001
pdbSet            Diffuse dThicknessEpi       0.10        ;# default 0.1

# Numerics: Temperature Steps
pdbSet            Diffuse MaxGrowthStep       4.0         ;# default 4.0
pdbSet            Diffuse delT                10.0        ;# default 10
pdbSet            Diffuse delNT               50.0        ;# default 50
pdbSet            Diffuse delTox              50.0        ;# default 50

# Physics: Turn on dopant clusters
pdbSet            Si B    ActiveModel         Transient
pdbSet            Si P    ActiveModel         Transient
pdbSet            Si As   ActiveModel         Transient
pdbSet            Si In   ActiveModel         Transient
pdbSet            Si Sb   ActiveModel         Transient

# Physics: Turn on interstitial clusters, and use ifactors and vfactors of AdvancedCalibration.
pdbSet            Si Int  ClusterModel        1Moment
pdbSetBoolean     AdvCal_IFactorsVFactorsZero 0

# Numerics: Default errors for point defects
pdbSetDouble      Si Int  Abs.Error           1.0e7       ;# default 1.0e7
pdbSetDouble      Si Int  Rel.Error           1.0e-4      ;# default 1.0e-4
pdbSetDouble      Si Int  Transient.Rel.Error 1.0e-2      ;# default 1.0e-2
pdbSetDouble      Si Vac  Abs.Error           1.0e7       ;# default 1.0e7
pdbSetDouble      Si Vac  Rel.Error           1.0e-4      ;# default 1.0e-4
pdbSetDouble      Si Vac  Transient.Rel.Error 1.0e-2      ;# default 1.0e-2

LogFile PrintCommands
LogFile "- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -"
}}
Params&Command.119 String {mgoals run.only  accuracy= 1.0000000000000000818031e-05<um>}
