Params&Command.52 String {fproc run.only Use_As3P_clusters { {mat Si} } {
    solution name=As3P add ifpresent = "Arsenic Phosphorus" !negative store
    pdbSetString  $mat As3P EquationProc As3P_eq
    pdbSetDouble  $mat As3P_k1 {[Arr 2e-13 2.0]}
    pdbSetDouble  $mat As3P_k2  5e20
}}
Params&Command.53 String {fproc run.only As3P_eq { mat sol } {
    set k1 [pdbDelayDouble $mat As3P_k1]
    set k2 [pdbDelayDouble $mat As3P_k2]
    set Lc [expr int([pdbGetDouble $mat As Lc])]
    set eqn       "$k1 * (As${Lc} * PActive * Noni - $k2 * As3P)"
    term          $mat name=form_As3P store add eqn = $eqn
    pdbSetString  $mat As3P        Equation "ddt(As3P) - form_As3P"
    AddEqnTerm    $mat Phosphorus  form_As3P
    AddEqnTerm    $mat As${Lc}     form_As3P
    AddToTerm     $mat PTotal     "As3P"
    AddToTerm     $mat AsTotal    "3*As3P"
}}
Params&Command.54 String {solution run.only       add      ifpresent= "Phosphorus" name= "P2trap" !negative store}
Params&Command.55 String {fproc run.only P2trap_eq { mat sol } {
  if { [pdbGet $mat P BoundaryCondition] == "ThreePhaseSegregation" } {
    set m1 [FirstMat $mat]
    set m2 [SecondMat $mat]
    set k1 [_MoleFraction::Param $mat P2trap k1]
    set k2 [_MoleFraction::Param $mat P2trap k2]
    set eqn       "$k1 * (Phosphorus * Phosphorus - $k2 * P2trap * (1.0+ReactionSpeed*1e12))"
    term          $m1 /$m2 name=form_P2trap store add eqn = $eqn
    pdbSetString  $mat P2trap       Equation "ddt(P2trap) - form_P2trap"
    AddEqnTerm    $mat Phosphorus  "2*form_P2trap"
    AddToTerm     $mat PTotal      "2*P2trap"
  } else {
    pdbSetString  $mat P2trap       Equation "ddt(P2trap) - 0"
    printWarning  "Solution P2trap is not necessary and can be set to nosolve."
  }
}}
Params&Command.56 String {fproc run.only SiGe_and_Stress_Effect { Ge_Chem_Eff Stress_Eff Segreg_Model Strained_Overlayer } {

LogFile "- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -"
LogFile "SiGe_and_Stress_Effect $Ge_Chem_Eff $Stress_Eff $Segreg_Model $Strained_Overlayer"

    pdbSetBoolean     AdvancedCalibration.Stress.Eff $Stress_Eff

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2.14.1: Chemical SiGe alloy effect ---------------------------------------------------------

    if {$Ge_Chem_Eff} {
    # Parameter interpolation for SiGe alloy
      pdbSet          Si        Skip.Parameter.Interpolation 0
      pdbSet          Ge        Skip.Parameter.Interpolation 0
      pdbSet          Mechanics Lattice.Constant.Mismatch    1
      pdbSet          Mechanics Parameter.Interpolation      1
    } else {
      pdbSet          Si        Skip.Parameter.Interpolation 1
      pdbSet          Ge        Skip.Parameter.Interpolation 1
      pdbSet          Mechanics Lattice.Constant.Mismatch    0
      pdbSet          Mechanics Parameter.Interpolation      0
    }

    LogFile !PrintCommands

    alias Nit_SiGe    Nitride_SiliconGermanium

# 2.14.1.1: Potential

    pdbSetBoolean     SiGe Potential Poisson 1
    pdbSet            SiGe Potential Use.DOS 0

    fproc _Potential::SiGe::ni { } { ;# including factorNcNvTerm
      return "(1.0-xMoleFraction*0.705)*[pdbDelayDouble Si Potential ni]"
    }

    pdbSetFunction    SiGe Potential ni.Function    {[_Potential::SiGe::ni]}
    pdbSetFunction    SiGe Potential delEg.Function {[_Potential::SiGe::delEg]}

# 2.14.1.2: Point defects

    pdbSetString      SiGe Int Log.Interpolated.Params  { Cstar D Di ChargeStates ChargeStatesScale D.Inter BulkRecFac }
    pdbSetString      SiGe Vac Log.Interpolated.Params  { Cstar D Dv ChargeStates ChargeStatesScale D.Inter }

    pdbSet            SiGe Int Cstar.X2 10.0
    pdbSet            SiGe Int Di.X2    3.0e-3
    pdbSet            SiGe Int D.X2     { -1 3.0e-3
                                           0 3.0e-3
                                           1 3.0e-3
                                           2 3.0e-3 }

    pdbSet            SiGe Vac Dv.X2    {[Arr 1.09e12 3.22]}
    pdbSet            SiGe Vac D.X2     { -1 {[Arr 1.09e12 3.22]}
                                           0 {[Arr 1.09e12 3.22]}
                                           1 {[Arr 1.09e12 3.22]}
                                           2 {[Arr 1.09e12 3.22]} }

# IV-recombination scaling factor (applied in 2.14.5):

    pdbSetDouble      Si   Int BulkRecFac    1.0
    pdbSetDouble      Ge   Int BulkRecFac    1.0
    pdbSetDouble      SiGe Int BulkRecFac.X2 {[Arr 4.0e3 -1.0]}

    pdbSetString      Ox_SiGe  Int Log.Interpolated.Params    { Ksurf theta }
    pdbSetString      Ox_SiGe  Vac Log.Interpolated.Params    { Ksurf }
    pdbSetString      Gas_SiGe Int Log.Interpolated.Params    { Ksurf }
    pdbSetString      Gas_SiGe Vac Log.Interpolated.Params    { Ksurf }
    pdbSetString      Nit_SiGe Int Log.Interpolated.Params    { Ksurf }
    pdbSetString      Nit_SiGe Vac Log.Interpolated.Params    { Ksurf }

    pdbSet            Ox_SiGe  Int Ksurf.X2 {[pdbDelayDouble SiGe I Di.X2]}
    pdbSet            Ox_SiGe  Vac Ksurf.X2 {[pdbDelayDouble SiGe V Dv.X2]}
    pdbSet            Gas_SiGe Int Ksurf.X2 {[pdbDelayDouble SiGe I Di.X2]}
    pdbSet            Gas_SiGe Vac Ksurf.X2 {[pdbDelayDouble SiGe V Dv.X2]}
    pdbSet            Nit_SiGe Int Ksurf.X2 {[pdbDelayDouble SiGe I Di.X2]}
    pdbSet            Nit_SiGe Vac Ksurf.X2 {[pdbDelayDouble SiGe V Dv.X2]}

    pdbSetString      SiGe ICluster Log.Interpolated.Params   { Ikfi Ikfc Ikr }

    pdbSet            SiGe ICluster Ikfi.X2  {[pdbDelayDouble SiGe I Di.X2]}
    pdbSet            SiGe ICluster Ikfc.X2  {[pdbDelayDouble SiGe I Di.X2]}
    pdbSet            SiGe ICluster Ikr.X2   1.0e-4


# 2.14.1.3: SiGe Interdiffusion

    pdbSet            Si   Int D.Inter    {[Arr 1400       4.95]}
    pdbSet            Ge   Int D.Inter    {[Arr  353       4.28]}
    pdbSet            SiGe Int D.Inter.X2 {[Arr    0.0016 -0.29]}

    pdbSet            Si   Vac D.Inter    {[ArrBreak 240 4.69 2343.304 4.94 1000]}
    pdbSet            Ge   Vac D.Inter    {[Arr   41.6     3.32]}
    pdbSet            SiGe Vac D.Inter.X2 {[Arr    2.6e5   1.52]}

    term              Si   name=InterIDiffFactor store add eqn = "1.0"
    term              Si   name=InterVDiffFactor store add eqn = "1.0"

    pdbSet            Si   Ge Interdiffusion.Drift 0
    pdbSet            Ge   Si Interdiffusion.Drift 0
	
    if {$Ge_Chem_Eff} {
      pdbSet          Si   Ge DiffModel   Interdiffusion
      pdbSet          Ge   Si DiffModel   Interdiffusion
    } else {
      pdbSet          Si   Ge DiffModel   Constant
      pdbSet          Ge   Si DiffModel   Constant
    }


# 2.14.1.4: Boron

    pdbSetString      SiGe B Defects "Int"

# Boron diffusion in SiGe
    pdbSetString      SiGe B Int Not.Interpolated.Params    { ChargePair }
    pdbSetString      SiGe B Int Log.Interpolated.Params    { D lambdaK }

    pdbSet            SiGe B Int lambdaK.X2   0.02
    pdbSet            SiGe B Int D.X2         {  0 8.0e-3
                                                 1 8.0e-3
                                                 2 8.0e-3 }

# Boron clustering in SiGe
    pdbSetString      SiGe B Log.Interpolated.Params    { Solubility TotSolubility CluRate }

    pdbSet            SiGe B Solubility.X2    {[Arr 840 0.5]}
    pdbSet            SiGe B TotSolubility.X2 {[Arr 840 0.5]}
    pdbSet            SiGe B CluRate.X2       {[Arr 1.2e-5 1.75]}

# Boron segregation at Ox/SiGe
    pdbSetSwitch      Ox_SiGe B BoundaryCondition ThreePhaseSegregation

    pdbSetString      Ox_SiGe B Log.Interpolated.Params    { TrappingRate_SiliconGermanium TrappingRate_Oxide
                                                             EmissionRate_SiliconGermanium EmissionRate_Oxide
                                                             CMax Segregation Transfer }

# Boron mcdfactor
    fproc mcdfactor_Boron { Energy Dose } {
      set dfSi 0.8
      set dfGe 1.0
      if { [pdbGet  Si  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $dfSi }
      set xMoleFraction "(Germanium/(0.5*((-2.332e22*Germanium+2.5e45)^0.5+5.e22)))"
      return "($xMoleFraction*$dfGe+(1-$xMoleFraction)*$dfSi)"
    }
    fproc mcdfactor_Germanium_Boron { Energy Dose } {
      set dfSi 0.8
      set dfGe 1.0
      if { [pdbGet  Ge  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $dfGe }
      set xMoleFraction "(1.0-Silicon/(0.5*((2.332e22*Silicon+1.951e45)^0.5+4.417e22)))"
      return "($xMoleFraction*$dfGe+(1-$xMoleFraction)*$dfSi)"
    }

# Boron initial activation after implant
    fproc acinit_Boron { Energy Dose } {
      if { $Dose<1e14 }       { set acSi 8.0e18 
      } elseif { $Dose>1e15 } { set acSi 8.0e17
      } else                  { set acSi [expr 8.0e32/$Dose] }
      set acGe [pdbGet Ge B AcInit]
      if { [pdbGet  Si  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $acSi }
      set xMoleFraction "(Germanium/(0.5*((-2.332e22*Germanium+2.5e45)^0.5+5.e22)))"
      return "($acSi*(1-$xMoleFraction)+$acGe*$xMoleFraction)"
    }
    fproc acinit_BF2 { Energy Dose } {
      if { $Dose<1e14 }       { set acSi 8.0e18 
      } elseif { $Dose>1e15 } { set acSi 8.0e17
      } else                  { set acSi [expr 8.0e32/$Dose] }
      set acGe [pdbGet Ge B AcInit]
      if { [pdbGet  Si  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $acSi }
      set xMoleFraction "(Germanium/(0.5*((-2.332e22*Germanium+2.5e45)^0.5+5.e22)))"
      return "($acSi*(1-$xMoleFraction)+$acGe*$xMoleFraction)"
    }

    fproc aminit_Boron { Energy Dose } {
      set amSi [pdbGet Si B AmInit]
      set amGe [pdbGet Ge B AmInit] 
      if { [pdbGet  Si  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $amSi }
      set xMoleFraction "(Germanium/(0.5*((-2.332e22*Germanium+2.5e45)^0.5+5.e22)))"
      return "(exp(log($amSi)*(1-$xMoleFraction)+log($amGe)*($xMoleFraction)))"
    }
    fproc aminit_BF2 { Energy Dose } {
      set amSi [pdbGet Si B AmInit]
      set amGe [pdbGet Ge B AmInit] 
      if { [pdbGet  Si  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $amSi }
      set xMoleFraction "(Germanium/(0.5*((-2.332e22*Germanium+2.5e45)^0.5+5.e22)))"
      return "(exp(log($amSi)*(1-$xMoleFraction)+log($amGe)*($xMoleFraction)))"
    }

    fproc acinit_Germanium_Boron { Energy Dose } {
      if { $Dose<1e14 }       { set acSi 8.0e18 
      } elseif { $Dose>1e15 } { set acSi 8.0e17
      } else                  { set acSi [expr 8.0e32/$Dose] }
      set acGe [pdbGet Ge B AcInit]
      if { [pdbGet  Ge  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $acGe }
      set xMoleFraction "(1.0-Silicon/(0.5*((2.332e22*Silicon+1.951e45)^0.5+4.417e22)))"
      return "($acSi*(1-$xMoleFraction)+$acGe*$xMoleFraction)"
    }
    fproc acinit_Germanium_BF2 { Energy Dose } {
      if { $Dose<1e14 }       { set acSi 8.0e18 
      } elseif { $Dose>1e15 } { set acSi 8.0e17
      } else                  { set acSi [expr 8.0e32/$Dose] }
      set acGe [pdbGet Ge B AcInit]
      if { [pdbGet  Ge  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $acGe }
      set xMoleFraction "(1.0-Silicon/(0.5*((2.332e22*Silicon+1.951e45)^0.5+4.417e22)))"
      return "($acSi*(1-$xMoleFraction)+$acGe*$xMoleFraction)"
    }

    fproc aminit_Germanium_Boron { Energy Dose } {
      set amSi [pdbGet Si B AmInit]
      set amGe [pdbGet Ge B AmInit] 
      if { [pdbGet  Ge  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $amGe }
      set xMoleFraction "(1.0-Silicon/(0.5*((2.332e22*Silicon+1.951e45)^0.5+4.417e22)))"
      return "(exp(log($amSi)*(1-$xMoleFraction)+log($amGe)*($xMoleFraction)))"
    }
    fproc aminit_Germanium_BF2 { Energy Dose } {
      set amSi [pdbGet Si B AmInit]
      set amGe [pdbGet Ge B AmInit] 
      if { [pdbGet  Ge  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $amGe }
      set xMoleFraction "(1.0-Silicon/(0.5*((2.332e22*Silicon+1.951e45)^0.5+4.417e22)))"
      return "(exp(log($amSi)*(1-$xMoleFraction)+log($amGe)*($xMoleFraction)))"
    }

# 2.14.1.5: Arsenic

    pdbSetString      SiGe As Defects "Int Vac"

# Arsenic diffusion in SiGe
    pdbSetString      SiGe As Int Not.Interpolated.Params    { ChargePair }
    pdbSetString      SiGe As Int Log.Interpolated.Params    { D lambdaK }

    pdbSetString      SiGe As Vac Not.Interpolated.Params    { ChargePair }
    pdbSetString      SiGe As Vac Log.Interpolated.Params    { D lambdaK }

    pdbSet            SiGe As Vac D.X2         { -2 {[Arr 9.e10 3.22]} 
                                                 -1 {[Arr 9.e10 3.22]} 
                                                  0 {[Arr 9.e10 3.22]} } 

# Arsenic clustering in SiGe
    pdbSetString      SiGe As Log.Interpolated.Params    { CluRate }

    pdbSet            SiGe As Solubility.X2    {[Arr 5.8e22   0.5]}
    pdbSet            SiGe As CluRate.X2       {[ArrBreak 1.31191 1.4 9.6e7 3.2 880]}

# Arsenic segregation at Ox/SiGe
    pdbSetSwitch      Ox_SiGe As BoundaryCondition ThreePhaseSegregation

    pdbSetString      Ox_SiGe As Log.Interpolated.Params    { TrappingRate_SiliconGermanium TrappingRate_Oxide
                                                              EmissionRate_SiliconGermanium EmissionRate_Oxide
                                                              CMax Segregation Transfer }

    pdbSet            Ox_SiGe As CMax.X2                          16.0

# Arsenic ifactor and mcdfactor
    fproc ifactor_Arsenic { Energy Dose } {
      set ifSi [expr 1.0 + 8.0*$Dose/($Dose+1.0e14)*$Energy*$Energy/($Energy*$Energy+4.0)]
      set ifGe 1.0
      if { [pdbGet  Si  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $ifSi }
      set xMoleFraction "(Germanium/(0.5*((-2.332e22*Germanium+2.5e45)^0.5+5.e22)))"
      return "($xMoleFraction*$ifGe+(1-$xMoleFraction)*$ifSi)"
    }
    fproc ifactor_Germanium_Arsenic { Energy Dose } {
      set ifSi [expr 1.0 + 8.0*$Dose/($Dose+1.0e14)*$Energy*$Energy/($Energy*$Energy+4.0)]
      set ifGe 1.0
      if { [pdbGet  Ge  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $ifGe }
      set xMoleFraction "(1.0-Silicon/(0.5*((2.332e22*Silicon+1.951e45)^0.5+4.417e22)))"
      return "($xMoleFraction*$ifGe+(1-$xMoleFraction)*$ifSi)"
    }

    fproc mcdfactor_Arsenic { Energy Dose } {
      set dfSi [expr 1.0+(1.0+($Energy*$Energy+20*$Energy+200.0)/(1.5*$Energy*$Energy+10.0))  *($Dose+1.0e13)/($Dose+5.0e13)]
      set dfGe 6.5
      if { [pdbGet  Si  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $dfSi }
      set xMoleFraction "(Germanium/(0.5*((-2.332e22*Germanium+2.5e45)^0.5+5.e22)))"
      return "($xMoleFraction*$dfGe+(1-$xMoleFraction)*$dfSi)"
    }
    fproc mcdfactor_Germanium_Arsenic { Energy Dose } {
      set dfSi [expr 1.0+(1.0+($Energy*$Energy+20*$Energy+200.0)/(1.5*$Energy*$Energy+10.0))  *($Dose+1.0e13)/($Dose+5.0e13)]
      set dfGe 6.5
      if { [pdbGet  Ge  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $dfGe }
      set xMoleFraction "(1.0-Silicon/(0.5*((2.332e22*Silicon+1.951e45)^0.5+4.417e22)))"
      return "($xMoleFraction*$dfGe+(1-$xMoleFraction)*$dfSi)"
    }


# 2.14.1.6: Phosphorus 

    pdbSetString      SiGe P Defects "Int Vac"

# Phosphorus diffusion in SiGe
    pdbSetString      SiGe P Int Not.Interpolated.Params    { ChargePair }
    pdbSetString      SiGe P Int Log.Interpolated.Params    { D lambdaK }

    pdbSet            SiGe P Int D.X2         { -2 {[Arr 8e13 2.13]}
                                                -1 {[Arr 8e13 2.13]}
                                                 0 {[Arr 8e13 2.13]} }
    pdbSet            SiGe P Int lambdaK.X2   {[Arr 3.3e4 0.5]}

    pdbSetString      SiGe P Vac Not.Interpolated.Params    { ChargePair }
    pdbSetString      SiGe P Vac Log.Interpolated.Params    { D lambdaK }

    pdbSet            SiGe P Vac D.X2         { -2 {[Arr 3e13 3.46]}
                                                -1 {[Arr 3e13 3.46]}
                                                 0 {[Arr 3e13 3.46]} }

# Phosphorus clustering in SiGe
    pdbSetString      SiGe P Log.Interpolated.Params    { CluRate }

    pdbSet            SiGe P Solubility.X2    {[pdbDelayDouble Si P Solubility]}
    pdbSet            SiGe P TotSolubility.X2 {[pdbDelayDouble Si P TotSolubility]}
    pdbSet            SiGe P CluRate.X2       {[Arr 1.0e10 3.3]}

# Phosphorus segregation at Ox/SiGe
    pdbSetSwitch      Ox_SiGe P BoundaryCondition ThreePhaseSegregation

    pdbSetString      Ox_SiGe P Log.Interpolated.Params    { TrappingRate_SiliconGermanium TrappingRate_Oxide
                                                             EmissionRate_SiliconGermanium EmissionRate_Oxide
                                                             CMax Segregation Transfer }

    pdbSet            Ox_SiGe P CMax.X2       {[Arr 0.0018 -0.25]}

# Phosphorus ifactor and mcdfactor
    fproc ifactor_Phosphorus { Energy Dose } {
      set ifSi [expr 1.0 + 1.0/$Energy]
      set ifGe 2.2
      if { [pdbGet  Si  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $ifSi }
      set xMoleFraction "(Germanium/(0.5*((-2.332e22*Germanium+2.5e45)^0.5+5.e22)))"
      return "($xMoleFraction*$ifGe+(1-$xMoleFraction)*$ifSi)"
    }
    fproc ifactor_Germanium_Phosphorus { Energy Dose } {
      set ifSi [expr 1.0 + 1.0/$Energy]
      set ifGe 2.2
      if { [pdbGet  Ge  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $ifGe }
      set xMoleFraction "(1.0-Silicon/(0.5*((2.332e22*Silicon+1.951e45)^0.5+4.417e22)))"
      return "($xMoleFraction*$ifGe+(1-$xMoleFraction)*$ifSi)"
    }

    fproc mcdfactor_Phosphorus { Energy Dose } {
      set dfSi 3.0
      set dfGe 7.0
      if { [pdbGet  Si  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $dfSi }
      set xMoleFraction "(Germanium/(0.5*((-2.332e22*Germanium+2.5e45)^0.5+5.e22)))"
      return "($xMoleFraction*$dfGe+(1-$xMoleFraction)*$dfSi)"
    }
    fproc mcdfactor_Germanium_Phosphorus { Energy Dose } {
      set dfSi 3.0
      set dfGe 7.0
      if { [pdbGet  Ge  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $dfGe }
      set xMoleFraction "(1.0-Silicon/(0.5*((2.332e22*Silicon+1.951e45)^0.5+4.417e22)))"
      return "($xMoleFraction*$dfGe+(1-$xMoleFraction)*$dfSi)"
    }

# 2.14.1.7: Antimony 

    pdbSetString      SiGe Sb Defects "Int Vac"

# Antimony diffusion in SiGe
    pdbSetString      SiGe Sb Int Not.Interpolated.Params    { ChargePair }

    pdbSetString      SiGe Sb Vac Not.Interpolated.Params    { ChargePair }
    pdbSetString      SiGe Sb Vac Log.Interpolated.Params    { D }

    pdbSet            SiGe Sb Vac D.X2     { -2 {[Arr 1.5e12 3.22]} 
                                             -1 {[Arr 1.5e12 3.22]} 
                                              0 {[Arr 1.5e12 3.22]} } 

# Antimony clustering in SiGe
    pdbSetString      SiGe Sb Log.Interpolated.Params    { CluRate }

    pdbSet            SiGe Sb Solubility.X2    {[pdbDelayDouble Si Sb Solubility]}
    pdbSet            SiGe Sb TotSolubility.X2 {[pdbDelayDouble Si Sb TotSolubility]}
    pdbSet            SiGe Sb CluRate.X2       {[Arr 1.0e12 2.2]}

# Antimony segregation at Ox/SiGe
    pdbSetSwitch      Ox_SiGe Sb BoundaryCondition Segregation

    pdbSetString      Ox_SiGe Sb Log.Interpolated.Params    { Segregation Transfer }

# Interpolation of AmInit
    fproc aminit_Antimony { Energy Dose } {
      set amSi [pdbGet Si Sb AmInit]
      set amGe [pdbGet Ge Sb AmInit] 
      if { [pdbGet  Si  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $amSi }
      set xM "(Germanium/(0.5*((-2.332e22*Germanium+2.5e45)^0.5+5.e22)))"
      return "($xM > 0.7727) ? 5.67e19 : (exp($xM*log(2.3e19)+(1.0-$xM)*log(6.4e20)+$xM*(1-$xM)*log(2.3)))" 
    }
    fproc aminit_Germanium_Antimony { Energy Dose } {
      set amSi [pdbGet Si Sb AmInit]
      set amGe [pdbGet Ge Sb AmInit] 
      if { [pdbGet  Ge  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $amGe }
      set xM "(1.0-Silicon/(0.5*((2.332e22*Silicon+1.951e45)^0.5+4.417e22)))"
      return "($xM > 0.7727) ? 5.67e19 : (exp($xM*log(2.3e19)+(1.0-$xM)*log(6.4e20)+$xM*(1-$xM)*log(2.3)))" 
    }

# 2.14.1.8: Fluorine

    pdbSetString      SiGe F Int         Not.Interpolated.Params { D ChargePair lambdaK kfKickOut
                                                                   kfFTM ClusterFormE ClusterCharge
                                                                   ClusterDegeneracy BindCluster
                                                                   CaptureRadiusFactor KfCluster }
    pdbSetString      SiGe F Vac         Not.Interpolated.Params { D ChargePair lambdaK kfKickOut }
    pdbSetString      SiGe F FluorineInt Not.Interpolated.Params { CaptureRadiusFactor }
    pdbSetString      SiGe F FluorineVac Not.Interpolated.Params { CaptureRadiusFactor }

# 2.14.1.9: Nitrogen

    pdbSetString      SiGe N Int         Not.Interpolated.Params { D ChargePair lambdaK kfKickOut
                                                                   kfFTM ClusterFormE ClusterCharge
                                                                   ClusterDegeneracy BindCluster
                                                                   CaptureRadiusFactor KfCluster }
    pdbSetString      SiGe N Vac         Not.Interpolated.Params { D ChargePair lambdaK kfKickOut }
    pdbSetString      SiGe N FluorineInt Not.Interpolated.Params { CaptureRadiusFactor }
    pdbSetString      SiGe N FluorineVac Not.Interpolated.Params { CaptureRadiusFactor }

# 2.14.1.10: Germanium and Silicon mcdfactor
   
    fproc mcdfactor_Germanium { Energy Dose } {
      set dfSi [expr 3.0*($Energy+80.0)/(2.5*$Energy+8.0)]
      set dfGe 2.4
      if { [pdbGet  Si  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $dfSi }
      set xMoleFraction "(Germanium/(0.5*((-2.332e22*Germanium+2.5e45)^0.5+5.e22)))"
      return "($xMoleFraction*$dfGe+(1-$xMoleFraction)*$dfSi)"
    }
    fproc mcdfactor_Germanium_Germanium { Energy Dose } {
      set dfSi [expr 3.0*($Energy+80.0)/(2.5*$Energy+8.0)]
      set dfGe 2.4
      if { [pdbGet  Ge  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $dfGe }
      set xMoleFraction "(1.0-Silicon/(0.5*((2.332e22*Silicon+1.951e45)^0.5+4.417e22)))"
      return "($xMoleFraction*$dfGe+(1-$xMoleFraction)*$dfSi)"
    }

    fproc mcdfactor_Silicon { Energy Dose } {
      set dfSi 1.3
      set dfGe 2.0
      if { [pdbGet  Si  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $dfSi }
      set xMoleFraction "(Germanium/(0.5*((-2.332e22*Germanium+2.5e45)^0.5+5.e22)))"
      return "($xMoleFraction*$dfGe+(1-$xMoleFraction)*$dfSi)"
    }
    fproc mcdfactor_Germanium_Silicon { Energy Dose } {
      set dfSi 1.3
      set dfGe 2.0
      if { [pdbGet  Ge  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation] } { return $dfGe }
      set xMoleFraction "(1.0-Silicon/(0.5*((2.332e22*Silicon+1.951e45)^0.5+4.417e22)))"
      return "($xMoleFraction*$dfGe+(1-$xMoleFraction)*$dfSi)"
    }

# 2.14.1.11: SiGe Oxidation

    pdbSetString      Ox_SiGe O2  100 Log.Interpolated.Params { BA0.l BA0.h C0.l C0.h L0.l L0.h }
    pdbSetString      Ox_SiGe O2  110 Log.Interpolated.Params { BA0.l BA0.h C0.l C0.h L0.l L0.h }
    pdbSetString      Ox_SiGe O2  111 Log.Interpolated.Params { BA0.l BA0.h C0.l C0.h L0.l L0.h }
    pdbSetString      Ox_SiGe H2O 100 Log.Interpolated.Params { BA0.l BA0.h C0.l C0.h L0.l L0.h }
    pdbSetString      Ox_SiGe H2O 110 Log.Interpolated.Params { BA0.l BA0.h C0.l C0.h L0.l L0.h }
    pdbSetString      Ox_SiGe H2O 111 Log.Interpolated.Params { BA0.l BA0.h C0.l C0.h L0.l L0.h }

# Germanium dependent oxidant diffusion
    if { $Ge_Chem_Eff } {
       term Ox   name=O2DiffFactor  store add eqn = "(Germanium+2.0e20)/2.0e20"
    } else {
       term Ox   name=O2DiffFactor  store add eqn = "1.0"
    }
    term Ox      name=H2ODiffFactor store add eqn = "1"

# Germanium segregation at Ox/SiGe
    term Ox  /Si name=GermaniumSegregationFactor store add eqn = {(1.0+ReactionSpeed*[Arr 1.0e26 2.0]*  (Germanium_Silicon>3.8e22 ? 0.0 : (1.0-Germanium_Silicon/3.8e22)) *                  (Germanium_Silicon>1.0e18 ? 1.0 : (Germanium_Silicon/1.0e18)) *                      (1.0-[pdbGet  Si  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation]))}
    term Ox  /Si name=GermaniumTransferFactor    store add eqn = {(1.0+ReactionSpeed*[Arr 1.0e35 4.0]*  (Germanium_Silicon>3.8e22 ? 0.0 : (1.0-Germanium_Silicon/3.8e22)) *                  (Germanium_Silicon>1.0e18 ? 1.0 : (Germanium_Silicon/1.0e18)) *                      (1.0-[pdbGet  Si  Skip.Parameter.Interpolation]))}


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2.14.2: Stress effect ----------------------------------------------------------------------

    set Vt_i {[simGetDouble Diffuse Vti]}
    set kT_i "($Vt_i/1.602177e-12)"
    set maxP [pdbGet SiGe Mechanics MaxPressure]

    if {$Stress_Eff} {
      set PR    ((Pressure>$maxP)?$maxP:((Pressure<(-$maxP))?(-$maxP):Pressure))
      set PR_Si ((Pressure_Silicon>$maxP)?$maxP:((Pressure_Silicon<(-$maxP))?(-$maxP):Pressure_Silicon))
    } else {
      set PR    0.0
      set PR_Si 0.0
    }

# 2.14.2.1: Potential

    if { $Stress_Eff } {
      pdbSet          Si Potential niMod StrainDependent
    } else {
      pdbSet          Si Potential niMod Constant
    }

    pdbSetDoubleArray Si Potential Ec Dilatational   { 1 -1.127   2 -1.127   3 -1.127 }
    pdbSetDoubleArray Si Potential Ec Deviatoric(1)  { 1 -9.16    2  0.0     3  0.0   }
    pdbSetDoubleArray Si Potential Ec Deviatoric(2)  { 1  0.0     2 -9.16    3  0.0   }
    pdbSetDoubleArray Si Potential Ec Deviatoric(3)  { 1  0.0     2  0.0     3 -9.16  }

    pdbSetDoubleArray Ge Potential Ec Dilatational   { 1  0.59    2  0.59    3  0.59  }
    pdbSetDoubleArray Ge Potential Ec Deviatoric(1)  { 1 -9.42    2  0.0     3  0.0   }
    pdbSetDoubleArray Ge Potential Ec Deviatoric(2)  { 1  0.0     2 -9.42    3  0.0   }
    pdbSetDoubleArray Ge Potential Ec Deviatoric(3)  { 1  0.0     2  0.0     3 -9.42  }

    pdbSetDoubleArray Si Potential Ev Dilatational   { 1 -2.46    2 -2.46 }
    pdbSetDoubleArray Si Potential Ev Deviatoric(1)  { 1  2.35    2  5.32 }
    pdbSetDoubleArray Si Potential Ev Deviatoric(2)  { 1  2.35    2  5.32 }

    pdbSetDoubleArray Ge Potential Ev Dilatational   { 1 -1.24    2 -1.24 }
    pdbSetDoubleArray Ge Potential Ev Deviatoric(1)  { 1  2.55    2  5.50 }
    pdbSetDoubleArray Ge Potential Ev Deviatoric(2)  { 1  2.55    2  5.50 }

# 2.14.2.2: Point defects

    pdbSetDouble      Si   Int idf    -0.142e-23
    pdbSetDouble      Si   Int icf    -1.211e-23
    pdbSetDouble      Si   Vac vdf     1.195e-23
    pdbSetDouble      Si   Vac vcf     1.324e-23
    pdbSetDouble      Ge   Int idf    -0.142e-23
    pdbSetDouble      Ge   Int icf    -2.800e-23
    pdbSetDouble      Ge   Vac vdf     8.000e-24
    pdbSetDouble      Ge   Vac vcf     1.324e-23

    pdbSetDouble      SiGe Vac vdf.X2  1.900e-23
    pdbSetDouble      SiGe Vac vcf.X2  2.000e-23

# In SiGe (if interpolated) the terms IntDiffFactor, VacDiffFactor, IntBulkRecombinationFactor, 
# IntCStarFactor, VacCStarFactor are redefined in section 2.14.5

    term Si name=IntDiffFactor  store add eqn = "exp($PR*[pdbDelayDouble Si Int idf]*$kT_i)"
    term Si name=VacDiffFactor  store add eqn = "exp($PR*[pdbDelayDouble Si Vac vdf]*$kT_i)"
    term Si name=IntCStarFactor store add eqn = "exp($PR*[pdbDelayDouble Si Int icf]*$kT_i)"
    term Si name=VacCStarFactor store add eqn = "exp($PR*[pdbDelayDouble Si Vac vcf]*$kT_i)"
    term Ge name=IntDiffFactor  store add eqn = "exp($PR*[pdbDelayDouble Ge Int idf]*$kT_i)"
    term Ge name=VacDiffFactor  store add eqn = "exp($PR*[pdbDelayDouble Ge Vac vdf]*$kT_i)"
    term Ge name=IntCStarFactor store add eqn = "exp($PR*[pdbDelayDouble Ge Int icf]*$kT_i)"
    term Ge name=VacCStarFactor store add eqn = "exp($PR*[pdbDelayDouble Ge Vac vcf]*$kT_i)"

    term Si name=IntBulkRecombinationFactor store add eqn = "[expr 4.0*3.14159*5e-8]*                  (IntDiffFactor*[pdbDelayDouble Si Int Di]+VacDiffFactor*[pdbDelayDouble Si Vac Dv])"
    term Ge name=IntBulkRecombinationFactor store add eqn = "[expr 4.0*3.14159*5e-8]*                  (IntDiffFactor*[pdbDelayDouble Ge Int Di]+VacDiffFactor*[pdbDelayDouble Ge Vac Dv])"
     
    term Ox  /Si name=IntInterfaceRecombinationFactor store add eqn = "IntDiffFactor_Silicon"
    term Ox  /Si name=VacInterfaceRecombinationFactor store add eqn = "VacDiffFactor_Silicon"
    term Nit /Si name=IntInterfaceRecombinationFactor store add eqn = "IntDiffFactor_Silicon"
    term Nit /Si name=VacInterfaceRecombinationFactor store add eqn = "VacDiffFactor_Silicon"
    term Ox  /Ge name=IntInterfaceRecombinationFactor store add eqn = "IntDiffFactor_Germanium"
    term Ox  /Ge name=VacInterfaceRecombinationFactor store add eqn = "VacDiffFactor_Germanium"
    term Nit /Ge name=IntInterfaceRecombinationFactor store add eqn = "IntDiffFactor_Germanium"
    term Nit /Ge name=VacInterfaceRecombinationFactor store add eqn = "VacDiffFactor_Germanium"

    term Si name=IClusterDissIntFactor store add eqn = "exp($PR*2.6e-23*$kT_i)"
    term Si name=C311DissIntFactor     store add eqn = "(1.0+2.0*xMoleFraction+0.5*xMoleFraction*xMoleFraction)*  exp($PR*1.6e-23*$kT_i)"
    term Si name=CLoopTransfer         store add eqn = "(1.0+25.*xMoleFraction+18.*xMoleFraction*xMoleFraction)*  exp($PR*3.3e-23*$kT_i)"   
    term Ge name=IClusterDissIntFactor store add eqn = "exp($PR*2.6e-23*$kT_i)"

# 2.14.2.3: Dopant diffusivity and activation

    term Si name=BoronIntDiffFactor              store add eqn = "exp(-2.394e-23*$PR*$kT_i)"
    term Si name=ArsenicIntDiffFactorPressure    store add eqn = "exp(-1.746e-23*$PR*$kT_i)"
    term Si name=ArsenicVacDiffFactorPressure    store add eqn = "exp(+1.800e-23*$PR*$kT_i)"
    term Si name=PhosphorusIntDiffFactorPressure store add eqn = "exp(-2.200e-23*$PR*$kT_i)"
    term Si name=PhosphorusVacDiffFactorPressure store add eqn = "VacDiffFactor*VacCStarFactor"
    term Si name=IndiumIntDiffFactor             store add eqn = "exp(-0.936e-23*$PR*$kT_i)"
    term Si name=AntimonyVacDiffFactor           store add eqn = "exp(+2.334e-23*$PR*$kT_i)"
    term Ge name=BoronIntDiffFactor              store add eqn = "exp(-2.394e-23*$PR*$kT_i)"
    term Ge name=ArsenicIntDiffFactor            store add eqn = "1"
    term Ge name=ArsenicVacDiffFactorPressure    store add eqn = "exp(+1.800e-23*$PR*$kT_i)"
    term Ge name=PhosphorusIntDiffFactor         store add eqn = "1"
    term Ge name=PhosphorusVacDiffFactor         store add eqn = "1"
    term Ge name=IndiumIntDiffFactor             store add eqn = "exp(-0.936e-23*$PR*$kT_i)"
    term Ge name=AntimonyVacDiffFactor           store add eqn = "exp(+2.334e-23*$PR*$kT_i)"

    pdbSetString      Si B  SS.Factor       "exp(3.636e-24*$PR*$kT_i)"        ;# = 0.2*3.0*0.303/5e22
    pdbSetString      Si B  Total.SS.Factor "exp(3.636e-24*$PR*$kT_i)"
    pdbSetString      Si B  Side.SS.Factor  "exp(3.636e-24*$PR_Si*$kT_i)"
    pdbSetString      Si As SS.Factor       "exp(-1.062e-24*$PR*$kT_i)"       ;# = 3.0*(-0.0177)/5e22
    pdbSetString      Si As Total.SS.Factor "exp(-1.062e-24*$PR*$kT_i)"
    pdbSetString      Si As Side.SS.Factor  "exp(-1.062e-24*$PR_Si*$kT_i)"
    pdbSetString      Si P  SS.Factor       "exp(4.596e-24*$PR*$kT_i)"        ;# = 3.0*0.0766/5e22
    pdbSetString      Si P  Total.SS.Factor "exp(4.596e-24*$PR*$kT_i)"
    pdbSetString      Si P  Side.SS.Factor  "exp(4.596e-24*$PR_Si*$kT_i)"
    pdbSetString      Si Sb SS.Factor       "exp(-5.796e-24*$PR*$kT_i)"       ;# = 0.6*3.0*(-0.161)/5e22
    pdbSetString      Si Sb Total.SS.Factor "exp(-5.796e-24*$PR*$kT_i)"
    pdbSetString      Si Sb Side.SS.Factor  "exp(-5.796e-24*$PR_Si*$kT_i)"

    pdbSetString      SiGe B  Not.Interpolated.Params { SS.Factor Total.SS.Factor Side.SS.Factor }
    pdbSetString      SiGe As Not.Interpolated.Params { SS.Factor Total.SS.Factor Side.SS.Factor }
    pdbSetString      SiGe P  Not.Interpolated.Params { SS.Factor Total.SS.Factor Side.SS.Factor }
    pdbSetString      SiGe Sb Not.Interpolated.Params { SS.Factor Total.SS.Factor Side.SS.Factor }

    pdbSetElement     Si B  Int ClusterFormE Boron   0.0
    pdbSetElement     Si B  Int ClusterFormE B2      "0.700+2.3*$PR/5.0e22/1.60213e-12"
    pdbSetElement     Si B  Int ClusterFormE B2I     "0.976+2.0*$PR/5.0e22/1.60213e-12"
    pdbSetElement     Si B  Int ClusterFormE B2I2    "1.108+1.7*$PR/5.0e22/1.60213e-12"
    pdbSetElement     Si B  Int ClusterFormE B3I     "-0.06+1.5*$PR/5.0e22/1.60213e-12"
    pdbSetElement     Si B  Int ClusterFormE B3I2    "0.140+1.2*$PR/5.0e22/1.60213e-12"
    pdbSetElement     Si B  Int ClusterFormE B3I3    "1.100+1.1*$PR/5.0e22/1.60213e-12"

    pdbSetElement     Si As Vac ClusterFormE Arsenic 0.0
    pdbSetElement     Si As Vac ClusterFormE As2     "0.45-0.10*$PR/5.0e22/1.60213e-12"
    pdbSetElement     Si As Vac ClusterFormE As2Vac  "1.10-0.05*$PR/5.0e22/1.60213e-12"
    pdbSetElement     Si As Vac ClusterFormE As3     "0.35-0.10*$PR/5.0e22/1.60213e-12"
    pdbSetElement     Si As Vac ClusterFormE As3Vac  "0.90-0.05*$PR/5.0e22/1.60213e-12"

# 2.14.2.4: Oxidation

    pdbSet            Ox Oxidant Stress.Dependent.Growth 1


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2.14.3: Dopant segregation at Si/SiGe interface --------------------------------------------

    if { $Segreg_Model } {
      pdbSetSwitch   Si B StressModel PDependent
      term           Si name=BoronIntSSFactor store add eqn = "exp($Vt_i*(0.5*(Pressure/5.11e10)))"
      term           Si name=BoronIntSPFactor store add eqn = "exp($Vt_i*(0.5*(Pressure/5.11e10)))"
      pdbSetSwitch   Ge B StressModel PDependent
      term           Ge name=BoronIntSSFactor store add eqn = "exp($Vt_i*(0.5*(Pressure/5.11e10)))"
      term           Ge name=BoronIntSPFactor store add eqn = "exp($Vt_i*(0.5*(Pressure/5.11e10)))"

      pdbSetSwitch   Si As StressModel PDependent
      term           Si name=ArsenicIntSSFactor store add eqn = "exp($Vt_i*(-0.2*xMoleFraction))"
      term           Si name=ArsenicIntSPFactor store add eqn = "exp($Vt_i*(-0.2*xMoleFraction))"
      term           Si name=ArsenicVacSSFactor store add eqn = "exp($Vt_i*(-0.2*xMoleFraction))"
      term           Si name=ArsenicVacSPFactor store add eqn = "exp($Vt_i*(-0.2*xMoleFraction))"
      pdbSetSwitch   Ge As StressModel PDependent
      term           Ge name=ArsenicIntSSFactor store add eqn = "exp($Vt_i*(-0.2*xMoleFraction))"
      term           Ge name=ArsenicIntSPFactor store add eqn = "exp($Vt_i*(-0.2*xMoleFraction))"
      term           Ge name=ArsenicVacSSFactor store add eqn = "exp($Vt_i*(-0.2*xMoleFraction))"
      term           Ge name=ArsenicVacSPFactor store add eqn = "exp($Vt_i*(-0.2*xMoleFraction))"

      pdbSetSwitch   Si P StressModel PDependent
      term           Si name=PhosphorusIntSSFactor store add eqn = "exp($Vt_i*(-0.6*xMoleFraction))"
      term           Si name=PhosphorusIntSPFactor store add eqn = "exp($Vt_i*(-0.6*xMoleFraction))"
      term           Si name=PhosphorusVacSSFactor store add eqn = "exp($Vt_i*(-0.6*xMoleFraction))"
      term           Si name=PhosphorusVacSPFactor store add eqn = "exp($Vt_i*(-0.6*xMoleFraction))"
      pdbSetSwitch   Ge P StressModel PDependent
      term           Ge name=PhosphorusIntSSFactor store add eqn = "exp($Vt_i*(-0.6*xMoleFraction))"
      term           Ge name=PhosphorusIntSPFactor store add eqn = "exp($Vt_i*(-0.6*xMoleFraction))"
      term           Ge name=PhosphorusVacSSFactor store add eqn = "exp($Vt_i*(-0.6*xMoleFraction))"
      term           Ge name=PhosphorusVacSPFactor store add eqn = "exp($Vt_i*(-0.6*xMoleFraction))"
    } else {
      pdbSetSwitch   Si B  StressModel None
      pdbSetSwitch   Si As StressModel None
      pdbSetSwitch   Si P  StressModel None
    }


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2.14.4: Strained overlayers ----------------------------------------------------------------
#         a) Usually not used.
#         b) Impact of thin oxide between Si and strained SiN is not clear from experiment.
#         c) Once turned on, the modification can be turned off only 'by hand'.

    if { $Strained_Overlayer && $Stress_Eff } {
      set Vt_i {[simGetDouble Diffuse Vti]}
      set kT_i "($Vt_i/1.602177e-12)"
      term Ox  /Si name=IntInterfaceCStarFactorOverlayers store add eqn =                     "exp(2.0e-23*Pressure_Oxide*$kT_i)"
      term Ox  /Si name=VacInterfaceCStarFactorOverlayers store add eqn =                     "exp(-2.0e-23*Pressure_Oxide*$kT_i)"
      term Nit /Si name=IntInterfaceCStarFactor           store add eqn =                     "exp(2.0e-23*Pressure_Nitride*$kT_i)"
      term Nit /Si name=VacInterfaceCStarFactor           store add eqn =                     "exp(-2.0e-23*Pressure_Nitride*$kT_i)"
    } 


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2.14.5: Definition of terms, to be interpolated or containing parameters to be interpolated.
#         This is a callback procedure, called in diffusion preprocessing after alloy setup. 

    fproc _AlloyCompound::redefineUserTerms {} {
    
      set Vt_i {[simGetDouble Diffuse Vti]}
      set kT_i "($Vt_i/1.602177e-12)"
      set maxP [pdbGet SiGe Mechanics MaxPressure]
      if { [pdbGetBoolean AdvancedCalibration.Stress.Eff] } {
        set PR    ((Pressure>$maxP)?$maxP:((Pressure<(-$maxP))?(-$maxP):Pressure))
      } else {
        set PR    0.0
      }

      set mlist [mater]
      if { [lsearch $mlist "Germanium"] == -1 } { lappend mlist "Germanium" }
      if { [lsearch $mlist "Silicon"]   == -1 } { lappend mlist "Silicon" }
      foreach mat $mlist {
        if { $mat == "Si" || $mat == "Ge" || [isLike $mat Si] || [isLike $mat Ge] } {
          term $mat name=IntDiffFactor  store add eqn = "exp($PR*[_MoleFraction::Param $mat Int idf]*$kT_i)"
          term $mat name=VacDiffFactor  store add eqn = "exp($PR*[_MoleFraction::Param $mat Vac vdf]*$kT_i)"
          term $mat name=IntCStarFactor store add eqn = "exp($PR*[_MoleFraction::Param $mat Int icf]*$kT_i)"
          term $mat name=VacCStarFactor store add eqn = "exp($PR*[_MoleFraction::Param $mat Vac vcf]*$kT_i)"
    
          term $mat name=IntBulkRecombinationFactor store add eqn = "[expr 4.0*3.14159*5e-8]*                  (IntDiffFactor*[_MoleFraction::Param $mat Int Di]+                                              VacDiffFactor*[_MoleFraction::Param $mat Vac Dv])*[_MoleFraction::Param $mat Int BulkRecFac]"

          term $mat name=BoronTClusterForwardFac store eqn = [_MoleFraction::TermParam BoronTClusterForwardFac $mat check]
          term $mat name=BoronDiffFactor         store eqn = [_MoleFraction::TermParam BoronDiffFactor         $mat check]
        }
      }
    }

LogFile PrintCommands
LogFile "- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -"
}}

}}

Params&Silicon&Vacancy&D {DoubleArray { 1 {[pdbGetDouble Si Vac Dv]} 0 {[pdbGetDouble Si Vac Dv]} -1 {[pdbGetDouble Si Vac Dv]} -2 {[pdbGetDouble Si Vac Dv]} }}
Params&Silicon&Vacancy&DoSPER Boolean 0
Params&Silicon&Vacancy&Dv Double {[Arr 0.03     1.80]}
Params&Silicon&Vacancy&Equation String {0 - (FluxPhosphorusVac) + ddt(VacNeutral * PActive * (( [expr [Arr 0.46 0.131] * [ArrBreak 1.000000e-18 0 1.8487e-30 -1.800 500]] +  [expr [Arr 1.7  0.3] * [ArrBreak 1.000000e-18 0 1.8487e-30 -1.800 500]] * Noni) * Noni + ([expr 1.0 * [ArrBreak 1.000000e-18 0 1.8487e-30 -1.800 500]]))) + ddt(Vac) - DiffVac  * EqVac * grad(ScaleVac) + RIV_VacInt + R_ICluster_Vac}
Params&Silicon&Vacancy&Total.Present Boolean 0
Params&SiliconCarbide&Mechanics&BulkModulus Double 220.552e10
Params&SiliconCarbide&Mechanics&ShearModulus Double 191.106e10
Params&SiliconCarbide&Mechanics&ThExpCoeff Double 3.25622e-6
Params&SiliconGermanium&CompoundInterp {DoubleArray { 1 {1} 0 {0} }}
Params&SiliconGermanium&LatticeConstant.X2 Double -0.026
Params&SiliconGermanium&Mechanics&MaxPressure Double 4.0e10

Params&TiNitride&Phosphorus&imp.table String Default/TiNitrideP_16e13-8e15.tab

For region Silicon_1, Silicon --> Silicon
For region Photoresist_1, Photoresist --> Photoresist

Params&Command.1 String {fset run.only Tsi {5.0} }
Params&Command.10 String {term run.only Ge name=VacBulkRecombinationFactor store add eqn = IntBulkRecombinationFactor}
Params&Command.100 String {fproc run.only mcdfactor_Silicon { Energy Dose } { return 1.3 }}
Params&Command.101 String {fproc run.only mcdfactor_Germanium_Silicon { Energy Dose } { return 2.0 }}
